The repo has a bug: some code path fails to unsubscribe a\n`'job:done'` handler, leaking subscribers. Fix it. Keep every public API and\nsignature unchanged; do not delete files; adding files is fine. Node.js\nbuiltins only, ESM, no external packages.\n\n`Bus` provides exactly: `on(type, handler)` (adds, returns `this`), `off(type,\nhandler)` (removes; unknown handler/type ignored; returns `this`),\n`emit(type, payload)`, `listenerCount(type)` (0 for unknown types). The same handler function registered twice on a type counts once.\n`emit` returns the count invoked, runs them in registration order, and\nsnapshots the list when it begins: a handler added during an emit runs only\nin later emits; one removed during an emit still runs later in that emit.\n\n`Scheduler(bus, options)`, `options = {now}`; validate `bus` first, then\n`now`. `bus` must provide `on`, `off`, `emit` as functions, else `TypeError`\n(`'bus must provide on, off and emit'`). `now` must be a function or undefined\n(default `Date.now`), else `TypeError` (`'now must be a function or\nundefined'`). `now` is called exactly: once per successful `start`, once per\njob-transitioning `cancel`, and once by the done handler when a payload lacks\na finite `atMs`. Nothing else invokes it.\n\n`schedule(id, runAtMs)`: validate `id` (non-empty string) then `runAtMs`\n(finite number), else `TypeError`. If the id maps to a non-terminal job throw\n`Error('duplicate job id')`. Creates exactly one `'job:done'` subscription and\nreturns `{id, runAtMs, status: 'pending', startedAt: null, endedAt: null,\ndurationMs: null}`. Rescheduling a terminal id replaces that record.\n\n`start(id)`: only a `pending` job moves to `running` with\n`startedAt = now()`, returning true; any other job returns false unchanged.\n\n`cancel(id)`: unknown or terminal jobs return false. A `pending` or `running`\njob moves to `cancelled`, sets `endedAt = now()`, returns true, and MUST\nunsubscribe its `'job:done'` handler, so a later `emit('job:done', {id})` is a\nno-op: status and `endedAt` stay unchanged, for started and never-started\njobs alike.\n\nEmitting `'job:done'` with payload `{id, atMs}` moves a non-terminal job with\nthat id to `done`, sets `endedAt = atMs` (or `now()` if `atMs` is not finite),\nunsubscribes, and sets `durationMs = endedAt - startedAt` if started, else\nnull. Terminal jobs, other ids and non-object payloads are ignored.\n\n`status(id)` returns the status string or null. `snapshot()` returns a fresh\narray of fresh view objects each call. `stats()` returns counts:\n`{total, pending, running, done, cancelled}`.\n\n\n\nNote: work carefully and double-check edge cases before finishing."}],"timestamp":1794045696865}}

Exported API: class Bus.\nexport class Bus {\n  constructor() {\n    this._handlers = new Map();\n  }\n\n  on(type, handler) {\n    if (!this._handlers.has(type)) this._handlers.set(type, new Set());\n    this._handlers.get(type).add(handler);\n    return this;\n  }\n\n  off(type, handler) {\n    const set = this._handlers.get(type);\n    if (set) set.delete(handler);\n    return this;\n  }\n\n  emit(type, payload) {\n    const set = this._handlers.get(type);\n    if (!set || set.size === 0) return 0;\n    let n = 0;\n    for (const handler of [...set]) {\n      handler(payload);\n      n++;\n    }\n    return n;\n  }\n\n  listenerCount(type) {\n    const set = this._handlers.get(type);\n    return set ? set.size : 0;\n  }\n}\n---scheduler---\n// Scheduler built on top of Bus. Exported API: class Scheduler.\n// See behavior.md for the invariants this module must hold.\nimport { Bus } from './bus.js';\n\nconst DONE = 'job:done';\n\nexport class Scheduler {\n  constructor(bus, options = {}) {\n    if (!bus || typeof bus.on !== 'function' || typeof bus.off !== 'function' || typeof bus.emit !== 'function') {\n      throw new TypeError('bus must provide on, off and emit');\n    }\n    if (options.now !== undefined && typeof options.now !== 'function') {\n      throw new TypeError('now must be a function or undefined');\n    }\n    this._bus = bus;\n    this._now = options.now === undefined ? Date.now : options.now;\n    /** @type {Map<string, any>} */\n    this._jobs = new Map();\n  }\n\n  schedule(id, runAtMs) {\n    if (typeof id !== 'string' || id.length === 0) {\n      throw new TypeError('id must be a non-empty string');\n    }\n    if (typeof runAtMs !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(runAtMs)) {\n      throw new TypeError('runAtMs must be a finite number');\n    }\n    if (this._jobs.has(id)) {\n      throw new Error('duplicate job id');\n    }\n    const job = {\n      id,\n      runAtMs,\n      status: 'pending',\n      startedAt: null,\n      endedAt: null,\n    };\n    const handler = (event) => {\n      if (!event || typeof event !== 'object') return;\n      if (event.id !== id) return;\n      job.status = 'done';\n      job.endedAt = Number.isFinite(event.atMs) ? event.atMs : this._now();\n      this._bus.off(DONE, handler);\n    };\n    job._handler = handler;\n    this._jobs.set(id, job);\n    this._bus.on(DONE, handler);\n    return this._view(job);\n  }\n\n  start(id) {\n    const job = this._jobs.get(id);\n    if (!job || job.status !== 'pending') return false;\n    job.status = 'running';\n    job.startedAt = this._now();\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  cancel(id) {\n    const job = this._jobs.get(id);\n    if (!job) return false;\n    if (job.status === 'done' || job.status === 'cancelled') return false;\n    if (job.startedAt === null) {\n      // Nothing has started yet: just mark it cancelled.\n      job.status = 'cancelled';\n      job.endedAt = this._now();\n      return true;\n    }\n    this._bus.off(DONE, job._handler);\n    job.status = 'cancelled';\n    job.endedAt = this._now();\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  status(id) {\n    const job = this._jobs.get(id);\n    return job ? job.status : null;\n  }\n\n  _view(job) {\n    const durationMs =\n      job.status === 'done' && job.startedAt !== null && job.endedAt !== null\n        ? job.endedAt - job.startedAt\n        : null;\n    return {\n      id: job.id,\n      runAtMs: job.runAtMs,\n      status: job.status,\n      startedAt: job.startedAt,\n      endedAt: job.endedAt,\n      durationMs,\n    };\n  }\n\n  snapshot() {\n    return [...this._jobs.values()].map((job) => this._view(job));\n  }\n\n  stats() {\n    const counts = { total: 0, pending: 0, running: 0, done: 0, cancelled: 0 };\n    for (const job of this._jobs.values()) {\n      counts.total++;\n      counts[job.status]++;\n    }\n    return counts;\n  }\n}\n"}],"details":{}}}

Every scheduled, non-terminal (`pending` or `running`) job holds exactly\n   one subscription on the `'job:done'` event.\n2. Once a job reaches a terminal state (`done` or `cancelled`) it holds ZERO\n   subscriptions; the scheduler must not leak subscribers.\n3. After a successful `cancel(id)`, emitting `'job:done'` for that id has no\n   effect on the job: its status stays `cancelled` and its `endedAt` is\n   unchanged.\n4. A `cancel` of a job that has been started (`running`) and a `cancel` of a\n   job that has never been started (`pending`) behave identically with respect\n   to invariants 1-3.\n\nThe suite under `test/` (run with `node --test`) encodes these invariants.\nOne of its cases is currently red: the shipped `src/scheduler.js` violates\nthe invariants. Every scheduled, non-terminal (`pending` or `running`) job holds exactly\n   one subscription on the `'job:done'` event.\n2. Once a job reaches a terminal state (`done` or `cancelled`) it holds ZERO\n   subscriptions; the scheduler must not leak subscribers.\n3. After a successful `cancel(id)`, emitting `'job:done'` for that id has no\n   effect on the job: its status stays `cancelled` and its `endedAt` is\n   unchanged.\n4. A `cancel` of a job that has been started (`running`) and a `cancel` of a\n   job that has never been started (`pending`) behave identically with respect\n   to invariants 1-3.\n\nThe suite under `test/` (run with `node --test`) encodes these invariants.\nOne of its cases is currently red: the shipped `src/scheduler.js` violates\nthe invariants. scheduler.test.mjs\n=== test/scheduler.test.mjs ===\nimport { test } from 'node:test';\nimport assert from 'node:assert/strict';\nimport { Bus } from '../src/bus.js';\nimport { Scheduler } from '../src/scheduler.js';\n\nconst tick = () => {\n  let t = 0;\n  const fn = () => t;\n  fn.advance = (n) => {\n    t += n;\n  };\n  return fn;\n};\n\ntest('schedule stores a pending job and counts exactly one subscriber per job', () => {\n  const bus = new Bus();\n  const sched = new Scheduler(bus);\n  const view = sched.schedule('a', 10);\n  assert.deepEqual(view, {\n    id: 'a',\n    runAtMs: 10,\n    status: 'pending',\n    startedAt: null,\n    endedAt: null,\n    durationMs: null,\n  });\n  assert.equal(bus.listenerCount('job:done'), 1);\n  sched.schedule('b', 20);\n  assert.equal(bus.listenerCount('job:done'), 2);\n});\n\ntest('done handler fires once and unsubscribes', () => {\n  const bus = new Bus();\n  const sched = new Scheduler(bus);\n  sched.schedule('a', 0);\n  assert.equal(bus.listenerCount('job:done'), 1);\n  assert.equal(sched.start('a'), true);\n  bus.emit('job:done', { id: 'a', atMs: 5 });\n  assert.equal(sched.status('a'), 'done');\n  assert.equal(bus.listenerCount('job:done'), 0);\n});\n\ntest('cancel on a running job unsubscribes so a later done is ignored', () => {\n  const bus = new Bus();\n  const sched = new Scheduler(bus, { now: tick() });\n  sched.schedule('a', 0);\n  sched.start('a');\n  assert.equal(sched.cancel('a'), true);\n  assert.equal(sched.status('a'), 'cancelled');\n  assert.equal(bus.listenerCount('job:done'), 0);\n  bus.emit('job:done', { id: 'a', atMs: 99 });\n  assert.equal(sched.status('a'), 'cancelled');\n});\n\ntest('cancel on a never-started pending job also unsubscribes', () => {\n  const bus = new Bus();\n  const sched = new Scheduler(bus, { now: tick() });\n  sched.schedule('a', 0);\n  assert.equal(sched.cancel('a'), true);\n  assert.equal(bus.listenerCount('job:done'), 0);\n  bus.emit('job:done', { id: 'a', atMs: 99 });\n  assert.equal(sched.status('a'), 'cancelled');\n});\n\ntest('cancel returns false for unknown or terminal jobs and keeps subscriber counts', () => {\n  const bus = new Bus();\n  const sched = new Scheduler(bus, { now: tick() });\n  assert.equal(sched.cancel('ghost'), false);\n  sched.schedule('a', 0);\n  sched.cancel('a');\n  assert.equal(sched.cancel('a'), false);\n  sched.schedule('b', 0);\n  sched.start('b');\n  bus.emit('job:done', { id: 'b', atMs: 1 });\n  assert.equal(sched.cancel('b'), false);\n});\n\ntest('duplicate ids and invalid arguments throw', () => {\n  const bus = new Bus();\n  const sched = new Scheduler(bus);\n  sched.schedule('a', 0);\n  assert.throws(() => sched.schedule('a', 1), /duplicate job id/);\n  assert.throws(() => sched.schedule('', 1), TypeError);\n  assert.throws(() => sched.schedule('b', Infinity), TypeError);\n  assert.throws(() => new Scheduler(bus, { now: 'nope' }), TypeError);\n  assert.throws(() => new Scheduler('not-a-bus'), TypeError);\n});\n\ntest('stats and snapshot reflect lifecycle', () => {\n  const bus = new Bus();\n  const now = tick();\n  const sched = new Scheduler(bus, { now });\n  sched.schedule('a', 0);\n  sched.schedule('b', 0);\n  now.advance(3);\n  sched.start('a');\n  now.advance(1);\n  bus.emit('job:done', { id: 'a', atMs: 4 });\n  sched.cancel('b');\n  assert.deepEqual(sched.stats(), { total: 2, pending: 0, running: 0, done: 1, cancelled: 1 });\n  const snap = sched.snapshot();\n  assert.equal(snap.length, 2);\n  assert.deepEqual(sched.snapshot().find((j) => j.id === 'b'), {\n    id: 'b',\n    runAtMs: 0,\n    status: 'cancelled',\n    startedAt: null,\n    endedAt: 4,\n    durationMs: null,\n  });\n});\n"}],"details":{}}}

{"type":"message_end","message":{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_8d664f64ab3d49528801c88f","toolName":"bash","content":[{"type":"text","text":"# Behavior invariants\n\n`src/bus.js` exports `Bus`, an EventEmitter-like pub/sub. `src/scheduler.js`\nexports `Scheduler`, which schedules jobs on a `Bus` and must hold ALL of the\nfollowing invariants at all times.\n\n1. Every scheduled, non-terminal (`pending` or `running`) job holds exactly\n   one subscription on the `'job:done'` event.\n2. Once a job reaches a terminal state (`done` or `cancelled`) it holds ZERO\n   subscriptions; the scheduler must not leak subscribers.\n3. After a successful `cancel(id)`, emitting `'job:done'` for that id has no\n   effect on the job: its status stays `cancelled` and its `endedAt` is\n   unchanged.\n4. A `cancel` of a job that has been started (`running`) and a `cancel` of a\n   job that has never been started (`pending`) behave identically with respect\n   to invariants 1-3.\n\nThe suite under `test/` (run with `node --test`) encodes these invariants.\nOne of its cases is currently red: the shipped `src/scheduler.js` violates\nthe invariants. Fix the module so every case is green without changing any\npublic API.\n---package---\n{\n  \"name\": \"event-bus-scheduler\",\n  \"version\": \"1.0.0\",\n  \"private\": true,\n  \"type\": \"module\"\n}\n---tests---\ntotal 12\ndrwxr-xr-x 2 iemma002 user 4096 Nov  7 05:01 .\ndrwxr-xr-x 4 iemma002 user 4096 Nov  7 05:01 ..\n-rw-r--r-- 1 iemma002 user 3520 Nov  7 05:01 scheduler.test.mjs\n=== test/scheduler.test.mjs ===\nimport { test } from 'node:test';\nimport assert from 'node:assert/strict';\nimport { Bus } from '../src/bus.js';\nimport { Scheduler } from '../src/scheduler.js';\n\nconst tick = () => {\n  let t = 0;\n  const fn = () => t;\n  fn.advance = (n) => {\n    t += n;\n  };\n  return fn;\n};\n\ntest('schedule stores a pending job and counts exactly one subscriber per job', () => {\n  const bus = new Bus();\n  const sched = new Scheduler(bus);\n  const view = sched.schedule('a', 10);\n  assert.deepEqual(view, {\n    id: 'a',\n    runAtMs: 10,\n    status: 'pending',\n    startedAt: null,\n    endedAt: null,\n    durationMs: null,\n  });\n  assert.equal(bus.listenerCount('job:done'), 1);\n  sched.schedule('b', 20);\n  assert.equal(bus.listenerCount('job:done'), 2);\n});\n\ntest('done handler fires once and unsubscribes', () => {\n  const bus = new Bus();\n  const sched = new Scheduler(bus);\n  sched.schedule('a', 0);\n  assert.equal(bus.listenerCount('job:done'), 1);\n  assert.equal(sched.start('a'), true);\n  bus.emit('job:done', { id: 'a', atMs: 5 });\n  assert.equal(sched.status('a'), 'done');\n  assert.equal(bus.listenerCount('job:done'), 0);\n});\n\ntest('cancel on a running job unsubscribes so a later done is ignored', () => {\n  const bus = new Bus();\n  const sched = new Scheduler(bus, { now: tick() });\n  sched.schedule('a', 0);\n  sched.start('a');\n  assert.equal(sched.cancel('a'), true);\n  assert.equal(sched.status('a'), 'cancelled');\n  assert.equal(bus.listenerCount('job:done'), 0);\n  bus.emit('job:done', { id: 'a', atMs: 99 });\n  assert.equal(sched.status('a'), 'cancelled');\n});\n\ntest('cancel on a never-started pending job assert.equal(bus.listenerCount('job:done'), 0);\n  bus.emit('job:done', { id: 'a', atMs: 99 });\n  assert.equal(sched.status('a'), 'cancelled');\n});\n\ntest('cancel returns false for unknown or terminal jobs and keeps subscriber counts', () => {\n  const bus = new Bus();\n  const sched = new Scheduler(bus, { now: tick() });\n  assert.equal(sched.cancel('ghost'), false);\n  sched.schedule('a', 0);\n  sched.cancel('a');\n  assert.equal(sched.cancel('a'), false);\n  sched.schedule('b', 0);\n  sched.start('b');\n  bus.emit('job:done', { id: 'b', atMs: 1 });\n  assert.equal(sched.cancel('b'), false);\n});\n\ntest('duplicate ids and invalid arguments throw', () => {\n  const bus = new Bus();\n  const sched = new Scheduler(bus);\n  sched.schedule('a', 0);\n  assert.throws(() => sched.schedule('a', 1), /duplicate job id/);\n  assert.throws(() => sched.schedule('', 1), TypeError);\n  assert.throws(() => sched.schedule('b', Infinity), TypeError);\n  assert.throws(() => new Scheduler(bus, { now: 'nope' }), TypeError);\n  assert.throws(() => new Scheduler('not-a-bus'), TypeError);\n});\n\ntest('stats and snapshot reflect lifecycle', () => {\n The repo has a bug: some code path fails to unsubscribe a\n`'job:done'` handler, leaking subscribers. Fix it. Keep every public API and\nsignature unchanged; do not delete files; adding files is fine. Node.js\nbuiltins only, ESM, no external packages.\n\n`Bus` provides exactly: `on(type, handler)` (adds, returns `this`), `off(type,\nhandler)` (removes; unknown handler/type ignored; returns `this`),\n`emit(type, payload)`, `listenerCount(type)` (0 for unknown types). The same handler function registered twice on a type counts once.\n`emit` returns the count invoked, runs them in registration order, and\nsnapshots the list when it begins: a handler added during an emit runs only\nin later emits; one removed during an emit still runs later in that emit.\n\n`Scheduler(bus, options)`, `options = {now}`; validate `bus` first, then\n`now`. `bus` must provide `on`, `off`, `emit` as functions, else `TypeError`\n(`'bus must provide on, off and emit'`). `now` must be a function or undefined\n(default `Date.now`), else `TypeError` (`'now must be a function or\nundefined'`). `now` is called exactly: once per successful `start`, once per\njob-transitioning `cancel`, and once by the done handler when a payload lacks\na finite `atMs`. Nothing else invokes it.\n\n`schedule(id, runAtMs)`: validate `id` (non-empty string) then `runAtMs`\n(finite number), else `TypeError`. If the id maps to a non-terminal job throw\n`Error('duplicate job id')`. Creates exactly one `'job:done'` subscription and\nreturns `{id, runAtMs, status: 'pending', startedAt: null, endedAt: null,\ndurationMs: null}`. Rescheduling a terminal id replaces that record.\n\n`start(id)`: only a `pending` job moves to `running` with\n`startedAt = now()`, returning true; any other job returns false unchanged.\n\n`cancel(id)`: unknown or terminal jobs return false. A `pending` or `running`\njob moves to `cancelled`, sets `endedAt = now()`, returns true, and MUST\nunsubscribe its `'job:done'` handler, so a later `emit('job:done', {id})` is a\nno-op: status and `endedAt` stay unchanged, for started and never-started\njobs alike.\n\nEmitting `'job:done'` with payload `{id, atMs}` moves a non-terminal job with\nthat id to `done`, sets `endedAt = atMs` (or `now()` if `atMs` is not finite),\nunsubscribes, and sets `durationMs = endedAt - startedAt` if started, else\nnull. Terminal jobs, other ids and non-object payloads are ignored.\n\n`status(id)` returns the status string or null. `snapshot()` returns a fresh\narray of fresh view objects each call. `stats()` returns counts:\n`{total, pending, running, done, cancelled}`.\n\n\n\nNote: work carefully and double-check edge cases before finishing."}],"timestamp":1794045696865},{"role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"toolCall","id":"call_937a58f9dbed4543a14faf82","name":"bash","arguments":{"command":"cd /lclhome/iemma002/workspace/bench/stage/t4-s1-pi-deepseek-v4-flash-sandbox && ls -la && echo \"---\" && cat src/bus.js && echo \"---scheduler---\" && cat src/scheduler.js"}}],"api":"anthropic-messages","provider":"bench","model":"deepseek-v4-flash","usage":{"input":244,"output":103,"cacheRead":2048,"cacheWrite":0,"totalTokens":2395,"cost":{"input":0,"output":0,"cacheRead":0,"cacheWrite":0,"total":0}},"stopReason":"toolUse","timestamp":1794045696880,"responseId":"msg_f6feddc753324e3686a0a294"},{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_937a58f9dbed4543a14faf82","toolName":"bash","content":[{"type":"text","text":"total 24\ndrwxr-xr-x 4 iemma002 user 4096 Nov  7 05:01 .\ndrwxr-xr-x 3 iemma002 user 4096 Nov  7 05:01 ..\n-rw-r--r-- 1 iemma002 user 1052 Nov  7 05:01 behavior.md\n-rw-r--r-- 1 iemma002 user   97 Nov  7 05:01 package.json\ndrwxr-xr-x 2 iemma002 user 4096 Nov  7 05:01 src\ndrwxr-xr-x 2 iemma002 user 4096 Nov  7 05:01 test\n---\n// Minimal EventEmitter-like Bus. Exported API: class Bus.\nexport class Bus {\n  constructor() {\n    this._handlers = new Map();\n  }\n\n  on(type, handler) {\n    if (!this._handlers.has(type)) this._handlers.set(type, new Set());\n    this._handlers.get(type).add(handler);\n    return this;\n  }\n\n  off(type, handler) {\n    const set = this._handlers.get(type);\n    if (set) set.delete(handler);\n    return this;\n  }\n\n  emit(type, payload) {\n    const set = this._handlers.get(type);\n    if (!set || set.size === 0) return 0;\n    let n = 0;\n    for (const handler of [...set]) {\n      handler(payload);\n      n++;\n    }\n    return n;\n  }\n\n  listenerCount(type) {\n    const set = this._handlers.get(type);\n    return set ? set.size : 0;\n  }\n}\n---scheduler---\n// Scheduler built on top of Bus. Exported API: class Scheduler.\n// See behavior.md for the invariants this module must hold.\nimport { Bus } from './bus.js';\n\nconst DONE = 'job:done';\n\nexport class Scheduler {\n  constructor(bus, options = {}) {\n    if (!bus || typeof bus.on !== 'function' || typeof bus.off !== 'function' || typeof bus.emit !== 'function') {\n      throw new TypeError('bus must provide on, off and emit');\n    }\n    if (options.now !== undefined && typeof options.now !== 'function') {\n      throw new TypeError('now must be a function or undefined');\n    }\n    this._bus = bus;\n    this._now = options.now === undefined ? Date.now : options.now;\n    /** @type {Map<string, any>} */\n    this._jobs = new Map();\n  }\n\n  schedule(id, runAtMs) {\n    if (typeof id !== 'string' || id.length === 0) {\n      throw new TypeError('id must be a non-empty string');\n    }\n    if (typeof runAtMs !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(runAtMs)) {\n      throw new TypeError('runAtMs must be a finite number');\n    }\n    if (this._jobs.has(id)) {\n      throw new Error('duplicate job id');\n    }\n    const job = {\n      id,\n      runAtMs,\n      status: 'pending',\n      startedAt: null,\n      endedAt: null,\n    };\n    const handler = (event) => {\n      if (!event || typeof event !== 'object') return;\n      if (event.id !== id) return;\n      job.status = 'done';\n      job.endedAt = Number.isFinite(event.atMs) ? event.atMs : this._now();\n      this._bus.off(DONE, handler);\n    };\n    job._handler = handler;\n    this._jobs.set(id, job);\n    this._bus.on(DONE, handler);\n    return this._view(job);\n  }\n\n  start(id) {\n    const job = this._jobs.get(id);\n    if (!job || job.status !== 'pending') return false;\n    job.status = 'running';\n    job.startedAt = this._now();\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  cancel(id) {\n    const job = this._jobs.get(id);\n    if (!job) return false;\n    if (job.status === 'done' || job.status === 'cancelled') return false;\n    if (job.startedAt === null) {\n      // Nothing has started yet: just mark it cancelled.\n      job.status = 'cancelled';\n      job.endedAt = this._now();\n      return true;\n    }\n    this._bus.off(DONE, job._handler);\n    job.status = 'cancelled';\n    job.endedAt = this._now();\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  status(id) {\n    const job = this._jobs.get(id);\n    return job ? job.status : null;\n  }\n\n  _view(job) {\n    const durationMs =\n      job.status === 'done' && job.startedAt !== null && job.endedAt !== null\n        ? job.endedAt - job.startedAt\n        : null;\n    return {\n      id: job.id,\n      runAtMs: job.runAtMs,\n      status: job.status,\n      startedAt: job.startedAt,\n      endedAt: job.endedAt,\n      durationMs,\n    };\n  }\n\n  snapshot() {\n    return [...this._jobs.values()].map((job) => this._view(job));\n  }\n\n  stats() {\n    const counts = { total: 0, pending: 0, running: 0, done: 0, cancelled: 0 };\n    for (const job of this._jobs.values()) {\n      counts.total++;\n      counts[job.status]++;\n    }\n    return counts;\n  }\n}\n"}],"isError":false,"timestamp":1794045697790},{"role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Let me examine the behavior.md and the test files."},{"type":"toolCall","id":"call_8d664f64ab3d49528801c88f","name":"bash","arguments":{"command":"cd /lclhome/iemma002/workspace/bench/stage/t4-s1-pi-deepseek-v4-flash-sandbox && cat behavior.md && echo \"---package---\" && cat package.json && echo \"---tests---\" && ls -la test/ && for f in test/*; do echo \"=== $f ===\"; cat \"$f\"; done"}}],"api":"anthropic-messages","provider":"bench","model":"deepseek-v4-flash","usage":{"input":1300,"output":133,"cacheRead":2304,"cacheWrite":0,"totalTokens":3737,"cost":{"input":0,"output":0,"cacheRead":0,"cacheWrite":0,"total":0}},"stopReason":"toolUse","timestamp":1794045697791,"responseId":"msg_b075a0e4acbc4f41ae1e9692"},{"role":"toolResult","toolCallId":"call_8d664f64ab3d49528801c88f","toolName":"bash","content":[{"type":"text","text":"# Behavior invariants\n\n`src/bus.js` exports `Bus`, an EventEmitter-like pub/sub. `src/scheduler.js`\nexports `Scheduler`, which schedules jobs on a `Bus` and must hold ALL of the\nfollowing invariants at all times.\n\n1.
